# World generation

Includes all worldgen type registries, built-in JSON entries and NBT structure templates, plus the three built-in dimension stems and generation mechanics. Resources in bundled optional datapacks are labeled by pack.

| ID | Feature | Vanilla source | Test | Vanilla | Ferraloom |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| worldgen.dimension.overworld | Generate the built-in `minecraft:overworld` dimension with its preset stem and generator | `WorldDimensions; WorldPresets; Level#OVERWORLD` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.dimension.nether | Generate the built-in `minecraft:the_nether` dimension with its preset stem and generator | `WorldDimensions; WorldPresets; Level#NETHER` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.dimension.end | Generate the built-in `minecraft:the_end` dimension with its preset stem and generator | `WorldDimensions; WorldPresets; Level#END` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.seed.parsing | Parse numeric seeds and string-hash seeds with vanilla signed integer semantics | `WorldOptions#parseSeed` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.seed.random-forks | Create positional and named worldgen random forks consistently across stages | `RandomState; PositionalRandomFactory; WorldgenRandom` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.biomes.multi-noise | Choose biomes from climate parameter points with vanilla nearest-parameter search | `MultiNoiseBiomeSource; Climate` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.biomes.end | Choose End center, highlands, midlands, barrens and small-island biomes from coordinates and noise | `TheEndBiomeSource` | | - | todo |

| worldgen.noise.ore-veins | Place ore veins, filler and raw ore with veininess and positional noise conditions | `OreVeinRule` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.noise.surface | Apply material condition and rule sequences using surface depth, biome and water context | `MaterialSystem; MaterialRules` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.noise.blending | Blend old and new chunk terrain and biome edges | `Blender; BlendingData` | | - | todo |
| worldgen.noise.retrogen | Apply below-zero retrogen and bedrock replacement to old chunks | `BelowZeroRetrogen` | | - | todo |
